   263	export function expectedFiles() {
   264	  const config = loadConfig();
   265	  const pins = loadPins();
   266	  const files = new Map();
   267	  files.set(".claude-plugin/marketplace.json", json(claudeMarketplace(config)));
   268	  files.set(".agents/plugins/marketplace.json", json(codexMarketplace(config)));
   269	
   270	  for (const plugin of config.plugins) {
   271	    const root = `plugins/${plugin.name}`;
   272	    let hasHooks = false;
   273	
   274	    if (plugin.kind === "vendored") {
   275	      const text = read("upstream", plugin.upstream, "SKILL.md");
   276	      const skillDir = `${root}/skills/${plugin.skill}`;
   277	      files.set(`${skillDir}/SKILL.md`, text);
   278	      files.set(`${skillDir}/agents/openai.yaml`, openaiYaml({
   279	        displayName: plugin.codexInterface.displayName,
   280	        shortDescription: plugin.codexInterface.shortDescription,
   281	        allowImplicit: !userOnly(text),
   282	      }));
   283	    } else if (plugin.kind === "authored") {
   284	      const srcSkills = path.join(repo, "src", plugin.name, "skills");
   285	      const onDisk = fs.existsSync(srcSkills) ? fs.readdirSync(srcSkills).filter((d) => !IGNORED_NAMES.has(d)) : [];
   286	      const listed = Object.keys(plugin.skills ?? {});
   287	      const unlisted = onDisk.filter((d) => !listed.includes(d));
   288	      const missing = listed.filter((d) => !onDisk.includes(d));
   289	      if (unlisted.length || missing.length) {
   290	        throw new Error(`${plugin.name}: skills in tools/plugins.json and src/${plugin.name}/skills/ differ (unlisted: ${unlisted.join(", ") || "none"}; missing: ${missing.join(", ") || "none"})`);
   291	      }
   292	      for (const [skill, spec] of Object.entries(plugin.skills ?? {})) {
   293	        const srcDir = path.join(srcSkills, skill);
   294	        let embedded = [];
   295	        for (const abs of walk(srcDir)) {
   296	          const rel = path.relative(srcDir, abs).split(path.sep).join("/");
   297	          const out = `${root}/skills/${skill}/`;
   298	          if (rel === "SKILL.md.tmpl") {
   299	            const tmpl = fs.readFileSync(abs, "utf8");
   300	            embedded = templateUpstreams(tmpl);
   301	            files.set(out + "SKILL.md", renderTemplate(plugin.name, tmpl, pins));
   302	          } else {
   303	            files.set(out + rel, fs.readFileSync(abs, "utf8"));
   304	          }
   305	        }
   306	        const listed = spec.upstreams ?? [];
   307	        if (embedded.join() !== listed.join()) {
   308	          throw new Error(`${plugin.name}:${skill}: the template embeds [${embedded.join(", ")}] but tools/plugins.json lists upstreams [${listed.join(", ")}]`);
   309	        }
   310	      }
   311	      const srcHooks = path.join(repo, "src", plugin.name, "hooks");
   312	      for (const abs of walk(srcHooks)) {
   313	        hasHooks = true;
   314	        files.set(`${root}/hooks/${path.relative(srcHooks, abs).split(path.sep).join("/")}`, fs.readFileSync(abs, "utf8"));
   315	      }
   316	      if (plugin.alwaysOn) {
   317	        const { chunks, manifest } = composeChunks(plugin, pins);
   318	        if (chunks.length !== TOTAL_CHUNKS) throw new Error(`${plugin.name}: alwaysOn lists ${chunks.length} chunks; the launcher expects ${TOTAL_CHUNKS}`);
   319	        chunks.forEach((c, i) => files.set(`${root}/hooks/chunks/${i + 1}.md`, c));
   320	        files.set(`${root}/hooks/chunks/manifest.json`, json(manifest));
   321	      }
   322	    } else {
   323	      throw new Error(`${plugin.name}: unknown kind ${plugin.kind}`);
   324	    }
   325	
   326	    files.set(`${root}/.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, json(claudePluginJson(plugin, config)));
   327	    files.set(`${root}/.codex-plugin/plugin.json`, json(codexPluginJson(plugin, config, hasHooks)));
   328	    files.set(`${root}/README.md`, pluginReadme(plugin, config));
   329	    const notice = noticeText(plugin, pins);
   330	    if (notice) files.set(`${root}/NOTICE.md`, notice);
   331	    for (const up of upstreamsShipped(plugin)) files.set(`${root}/LICENSES/${up}.LICENSE`, read("upstream", up, "LICENSE"));
   332	  }
   333	  return files;
   334	}
   335	
   336	// Files under a generated folder that the build would not write.
   337	export function strayFiles(expected = expectedFiles()) {
   338	  const strays = [];
   339	  for (const top of GENERATED_ROOTS) {
   340	    for (const abs of walk(path.join(repo, top))) {
   341	      const rel = path.relative(repo, abs).split(path.sep).join("/");
   342	      if (!expected.has(rel)) strays.push(rel);
   343	    }
   344	  }
   345	  return strays.sort();
   346	}
   347	
   348	export function staleFiles(expected = expectedFiles()) {
   349	  const stale = [];
   350	  for (const [file, text] of expected) {
   351	    let current = null;
   352	    try { current = read(file); } catch { /* missing */ }
   353	    if (current !== text) stale.push(file);
   354	  }
   355	  return stale;
   356	}
   357	
   358	export function writeAll(expected = expectedFiles()) {
   359	  for (const [file, text] of expected) {
   360	    const abs = path.join(repo, file);
   361	    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(abs), { recursive: true });
   362	    if (!fs.existsSync(abs) || fs.readFileSync(abs, "utf8") !== text) fs.writeFileSync(abs, text);
   363	  }
   364	}
   365	
   366	function removeEmptyDirs(dir) {
   367	  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return;
   368	  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   369	    if (entry.isDirectory()) removeEmptyDirs(path.join(dir, entry.name));
   370	  }
   371	  if (fs.readdirSync(dir).length === 0) fs.rmdirSync(dir);
   372	}
   373	
   374	export function prune(expected = expectedFiles()) {
   375	  const strays = strayFiles(expected);
   376	  for (const rel of strays) fs.rmSync(path.join(repo, rel));
   377	  for (const top of GENERATED_ROOTS) removeEmptyDirs(path.join(repo, top));
   378	  return strays;
   379	}
   380	
   381	if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
   382	  try {
   383	    const expected = expectedFiles();
   384	    if (process.argv.includes("--check")) {
   385	      const stale = staleFiles(expected);
   386	      const strays = strayFiles(expected);
   387	      if (stale.length) console.error("stale or missing generated files:\n  " + stale.join("\n  "));
   388	      if (strays.length) console.error("stray files under generated folders (run node tools/build.mjs --prune):\n  " + strays.join("\n  "));
   389	      if (stale.length || strays.length) process.exit(1);
   390	      console.log("generated files are current");
   391	    } else {
   392	      writeAll(expected);
   393	      console.log(`wrote ${expected.size} generated files`);
   394	      if (process.argv.includes("--prune")) {
   395	        for (const rel of prune(expected)) console.log(`deleted stray ${rel}`);
   396	      } else {
   397	        const strays = strayFiles(expected);
   398	        if (strays.length) console.warn("stray files under generated folders (delete with --prune):\n  " + strays.join("\n  "));
   399	      }
   400	    }
   401	  } catch (err) {
   402	    console.error(err.message);
   403	    process.exit(1);
   404	  }
   405	}

     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	// Install this marketplace with the real Claude Code and Codex CLIs, with no
     3	// keys and no sign-in, and check what each CLI loads. No model runs.
     4	//
     5	//   node tools/load-check.mjs [--claude <bin>] [--codex <bin>] [--only claude|codex] [--repo <path>]
     6	//
     7	// The repo's tracked and untracked-not-ignored files are copied into a fresh
     8	// git repo in a temp dir, which serves as the marketplace, so no CLI writes
     9	// into the working tree. Every CLI runs in throwaway homes that are deleted at
    10	// the end. Expected names, versions, and hook keys come from
    11	// tools/plugins.json and the generated plugins/*/hooks/hooks.json.
    12	//
    13	// Step one installs the authored plugins only and asserts that nothing else
    14	// comes with them. Step two installs the vendored plugins by name. Codex also
    15	// gets a catalog check through `codex app-server` (plugin/list, plugin/read,
    16	// hooks/list). Prints PASS and FAIL lines and exits 1 on any failure.
    17

    19	import fs from "node:fs";
    20	import os from "node:os";
    21	import path from "node:path";
    22	import readline from "node:readline";

    25	const CLI_TIMEOUT_MS = 180_000;
    26	const RPC_TIMEOUT_MS = 60_000;
    27	const STRIPPED_ENV = ["ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN", "OPENAI_API_KEY", "CODEX_API_KEY"];

    31	function parseArgs(argv) {
    32	  const opts = {
    33	    claude: "claude",
    34	    codex: "codex",
    35	    only: null,
    36	    repo: path.join(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), ".."),
    37	  };
    38	  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
    39	    const arg = argv[i];
    40	    const value = () => {
    41	      if (i + 1 >= argv.length) usage(`${arg} needs a value`);
    42	      return argv[++i];
    43	    };
    44	    if (arg === "--claude") opts.claude = value();
    45	    else if (arg === "--codex") opts.codex = value();
    46	    else if (arg === "--repo") opts.repo = value();
    47	    else if (arg === "--only") {
    48	      opts.only = value();
    49	      if (!["claude", "codex"].includes(opts.only)) usage(`--only takes claude or codex, not ${opts.only}`);
    50	    } else if (arg === "-h" || arg === "--help") usage();
    51	    else usage(`unknown argument ${arg}`);
    52	  }
    53	  opts.repo = path.resolve(opts.repo);
    54	  return opts;
    55	}

    57	function usage(message) {
    58	  if (message) console.error(message);
    59	  console.error("usage: node tools/load-check.mjs [--claude <bin>] [--codex <bin>] [--only claude|codex] [--repo <path>]");
    60	  process.exit(2);
    61	}

    65	let passed = 0;
    66	let failed = 0;
    67	
    68	function pass(name) {
    69	  passed++;
    70	  console.log(`PASS  ${name}`);
    71	}
    72	
    73	function fail(name, detail) {
    74	  failed++;
    75	  console.log(`FAIL  ${name}`);
    76	  if (detail) for (const line of String(detail).trimEnd().split("\n")) console.log(`      ${line}`);
    77	}
    78	
    79	function check(name, ok, detail) {
    80	  if (ok) pass(name);
    81	  else fail(name, typeof detail === "function" ? detail() : detail);
    82	  return ok;
    83	}

    85	// Thrown to stop one runtime's checks after a step that later steps need.
    86	class Abort extends Error {}
    87	
    88	const tail = (text, n = 20) => String(text ?? "").trimEnd().split("\n").slice(-n).join("\n");
    89	const sameSet = (a, b) => a.length === b.length && [...a].sort().join("\n") === [...b].sort().join("\n");
    90	const show = (list) => (list.length ? list.join(", ") : "(none)");

    92	// ---------------------------------------------------------------- temp dirs
    93	
    94	const tempRoots = [];
    95	const children = new Set();
    96	
    97	function cleanup() {
    98	  for (const child of children) {
    99	    try { child.kill("SIGKILL"); } catch { /* already gone */ }
   100	  }
   101	  // Codex writes shell snapshots with environment variables into CODEX_HOME.
   102	  for (const dir of tempRoots.splice(0)) fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   103	}
   104	
   105	process.on("exit", cleanup);
   106	for (const signal of ["SIGINT", "SIGTERM"]) process.on(signal, () => process.exit(130));

   108	function makeTempRoot() {
   109	  // realpath, because macOS reports /var/folders paths as /private/var/folders.
   110	  const dir = fs.realpathSync(fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "adhd-unslop-load-")));
   111	  tempRoots.push(dir);
   112	  return dir;
   113	}

   117	function baseEnv(extra) {
   118	  const env = { ...process.env };
   119	  for (const key of STRIPPED_ENV) delete env[key];
   120	  return { ...env, ...extra };
   121	}
   122	
   123	// Every CLI call except the app-server gets stdin from /dev/null.
   124	function run(bin, args, { env, cwd, timeout = CLI_TIMEOUT_MS } = {}) {
   125	  const r = spawnSync(bin, args, { env, cwd, stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"], encoding: "utf8", timeout, maxBuffer: 64 * 1024 * 1024 });
   126	  const output = `${r.stdout ?? ""}${r.stderr ?? ""}`;
   127	  let problem = null;
   128	  if (r.error) problem = r.error.code === "ETIMEDOUT" ? `timed out after ${timeout / 1000}s` : r.error.message;
   129	  else if (r.status !== 0) problem = `exit ${r.status ?? r.signal}`;
   130	  return { ok: !problem, problem, stdout: r.stdout ?? "", output };
   131	}
   132	
   133	// Run a CLI step that later steps depend on. PASS or FAIL, and abort on FAIL.
   134	function step(name, bin, args, opts) {
   135	  const r = run(bin, args, opts);
   136	  const cmd = [path.basename(bin), ...args].join(" ");
   137	  if (!check(name, r.ok, () => `$ ${cmd}\n${r.problem}\n${tail(r.output)}`)) throw new Abort(name);
   138	  return r;
   139	}
   140	
   141	function git(cwd, args) {
   142	  const r = spawnSync("git", args, { cwd, stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"], encoding: "utf8", maxBuffer: 64 * 1024 * 1024 });
   143	  if (r.status !== 0) throw new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${r.error?.message ?? tail(r.stderr)}`);
   144	  return r.stdout;
   145	}
   146	
   147	function cliVersion(bin, env) {
   148	  const r = run(bin, ["--version"], { env, timeout: 60_000 });
   149	  return r.ok ? r.stdout.trim() : `unavailable (${r.problem})`;
   150	}

   154	// Tracked plus untracked-not-ignored files, as tests/e2e/run.sh copies them.
   155	// Index entries deleted from the working tree are skipped.
   156	function copyRepo(repo, dest) {
   157	  const files = git(repo, ["ls-files", "-co", "--exclude-standard", "-z"]).split("\0").filter(Boolean);
   158	  fs.mkdirSync(dest, { recursive: true });
   159	  let copied = 0;
   160	  for (const rel of new Set(files)) {
   161	    const src = path.join(repo, rel);
   162	    let stat;
   163	    try { stat = fs.lstatSync(src); } catch { continue; }
   164	    const out = path.join(dest, rel);
   165	    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(out), { recursive: true });
   166	    if (stat.isSymbolicLink()) fs.symlinkSync(fs.readlinkSync(src), out);
   167	    else if (stat.isFile()) fs.copyFileSync(src, out);
   168	    else continue;
   169	    copied++;
   170	  }
   171	  const quiet = ["-c", "init.defaultBranch=main", "-c", "user.name=load-check", "-c", "user.email=load-check@example.invalid", "-c", "commit.gpgsign=false", "-c", "core.hooksPath=/dev/null"];
   172	  git(dest, [...quiet, "init", "-q"]);
   173	  git(dest, [...quiet, "add", "-A"]);
   174	  git(dest, [...quiet, "commit", "-q", "--no-verify", "-m", "load-check"]);
   175	  return copied;
   176	}

   178	// ---------------------------------------------------------------- expectations
   179	
   180	const snake = (event) => event.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, "$1_$2").toLowerCase();
   181	
   182	function listFiles(dir) {
   183	  const out = [];
   184	  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return out;
   185	  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   186	    const abs = path.join(dir, entry.name);
   187	    if (entry.isDirectory()) out.push(...listFiles(abs));
   188	    else out.push(abs);
   189	  }
   190	  return out;
   191	}
   192	
   193	function expectations(copy) {
   194	  const config = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(copy, "tools", "plugins.json"), "utf8"));
   195	  const mkt = config.marketplace.name;
   196	  const plugins = config.plugins.map((p) => {
   197	    const root = path.join(copy, "plugins", p.name);
   198	    const skills = p.kind === "vendored" ? [p.skill] : Object.keys(p.skills ?? {});
   199	    const required = [];
   200	    for (const skill of skills) required.push(`skills/${skill}/SKILL.md`);
   201	    // The hooks file Codex reads, as the Codex manifest names it, relative to the plugin root.
   202	    let hooksFile = "hooks/hooks.json";
   203	    try {
   204	      const codexManifest = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, ".codex-plugin", "plugin.json"), "utf8"));
   205	      if (typeof codexManifest.hooks === "string") hooksFile = codexManifest.hooks.replace(/^\.\//, "");
   206	    } catch { /* checked by the install steps */ }
   207	    const hookKeys = [];
   208	    const hooksPath = path.join(root, hooksFile);
   209	    if (fs.existsSync(hooksPath)) {
   210	      required.push(hooksFile);
   211	      const hooks = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(hooksPath, "utf8")).hooks ?? {};
   212	      for (const [event, groups] of Object.entries(hooks)) {
   213	        groups.forEach((group, g) => (group.hooks ?? []).forEach((_, h) => hookKeys.push(`${p.name}@${mkt}:${hooksFile}:${snake(event)}:${g}:${h}`)));
   214	      }
   215	    }
   216	    if (p.alwaysOn) {
   217	      required.push("hooks/chunks/manifest.json");
   218	      p.alwaysOn.forEach((_, i) => required.push(`hooks/chunks/${i + 1}.md`));
   219	    }
   220	    const sourceFiles = listFiles(root).map((abs) => path.relative(root, abs).split(path.sep).join("/"));
   221	    return {
   222	      name: p.name,
   223	      id: `${p.name}@${mkt}`,
   224	      kind: p.kind,
   225	      version: p.version,
   226	      skills,
   227	      fullSkills: skills.map((s) => `${p.name}:${s}`),
   228	      displayName: p.codexInterface?.displayName ?? null,
   229	      root,
   230	      required,
   231	      sourceFiles,
   232	      hooksFile,
   233	      hookKeys,
   234	    };
   235	  });
   236	  return { mkt, plugins };
   237	}

   239	// ---------------------------------------------------------------- cache checks
   240	
   241	const dirNames = (dir) => (fs.existsSync(dir) ? fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((n) => n !== ".DS_Store").sort() : []);
   242	
   243	// <cache>/<mkt>/ holds exactly the installed plugins, each with one version folder.
   244	function checkCacheLayout(label, mktCache, installed) {
   245	  const names = dirNames(mktCache);
   246	  check(`${label}: cache ${path.basename(mktCache)}/ holds only ${show(installed.map((p) => p.name))}`, sameSet(names, installed.map((p) => p.name)), `found: ${show(names)}`);
   247	  for (const p of installed) {
   248	    const versions = dirNames(path.join(mktCache, p.name));
   249	    check(`${label}: cache ${p.name}/ holds only ${p.version}/`, sameSet(versions, [p.version]), `found: ${show(versions)}`);
   250	  }
   251	}
   252	
   253	// The installed copy has the required files, and every shipped file byte for byte.
   254	// Extra files are allowed, so a CLI that adds metadata does not fail the check.
   255	function checkInstalledFiles(label, p, dir) {
   256	  const missing = p.required.filter((rel) => !fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, rel)));
   257	  check(`${label}: ${p.name} install has ${p.required.length} required files`, missing.length === 0, `missing under ${dir}:\n${missing.join("\n")}`);
   258	  const differ = p.sourceFiles.filter((rel) => {
   259	    const out = path.join(dir, rel);
   260	    return !fs.existsSync(out) || !fs.readFileSync(out).equals(fs.readFileSync(path.join(p.root, rel)));
   261	  });
   262	  check(`${label}: ${p.name} install matches all ${p.sourceFiles.length} shipped files`, p.sourceFiles.length > 0 && differ.length === 0, `missing or different under ${dir}:\n${differ.join("\n") || "(no shipped files found)"}`);
   263	}

   265	// ---------------------------------------------------------------- Claude Code
   266	
   267	function parseJson(text, what) {
   268	  const trimmed = text.trim();
   269	  const start = [trimmed.indexOf("["), trimmed.indexOf("{")].filter((i) => i >= 0).sort((a, b) => a - b)[0];
   270	  try {
   271	    return JSON.parse(start > 0 ? trimmed.slice(start) : trimmed);
   272	  } catch (err) {
   273	    throw new Error(`${what}: not JSON (${err.message}):\n${tail(text)}`);
   274	  }
   275	}
   276	
   277	function claudeList(bin, opts, label) {
   278	  const r = step(`${label}: claude plugin list --json`, bin, ["plugin", "list", "--json"], opts);
   279	  const list = parseJson(r.stdout, "claude plugin list --json");
   280	  if (!Array.isArray(list)) throw new Error(`claude plugin list --json: expected an array, got ${typeof list}`);
   281	  return list;
   282	}
   283	
   284	function checkClaudeInstalled(label, list, expected, mkt, configDir) {
   285	  const ours = list.filter((e) => String(e.id).endsWith(`@${mkt}`));
   286	  check(`${label}: installed from ${mkt}: exactly ${show(expected.map((p) => p.name))}`, sameSet(ours.map((e) => e.id), expected.map((p) => p.id)), `installed: ${show(ours.map((e) => e.id))}`);
   287	  for (const p of expected) {
   288	    const entry = ours.find((e) => e.id === p.id);
   289	    if (!entry) continue;
   290	    check(`${label}: ${p.id} version ${p.version}`, entry.version === p.version, `listed version: ${entry.version}`);
   291	    const errors = entry.errors ?? [];
   292	    check(`${label}: ${p.id} reports no errors`, Array.isArray(errors) && errors.length === 0, JSON.stringify(errors, null, 2));
   293	    check(`${label}: ${p.id} is enabled`, entry.enabled !== false, `enabled: ${entry.enabled}`);
   294	    const expectedPath = path.join(configDir, "plugins", "cache", mkt, p.name, p.version);
   295	    check(`${label}: ${p.id} installPath is the versioned cache copy`, path.resolve(entry.installPath ?? "") === expectedPath, `installPath: ${entry.installPath}\nexpected:    ${expectedPath}`);
   296	    if (entry.installPath) checkInstalledFiles(label, p, entry.installPath);
   297	  }
   298	  checkCacheLayout(label, path.join(configDir, "plugins", "cache", mkt), expected);
   299	}
   300	
   301	function runClaude(bin, copy, exp, tmp) {
   302	  const home = path.join(tmp, "claude-home");
   303	  const configDir = path.join(tmp, "claude-config");
   304	  fs.mkdirSync(home, { recursive: true });
   305	  fs.mkdirSync(configDir, { recursive: true });
   306	  const env = baseEnv({ HOME: home, USERPROFILE: home, CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR: configDir });
   307	  const opts = { env, cwd: copy };
   308	  console.log(`claude: ${cliVersion(bin, env)}`);
   309	
   310	  const first = exp.plugins.filter((p) => p.kind === "authored");
   311	  const second = exp.plugins.filter((p) => p.kind !== "authored");
   312	  try {
   313	    for (const target of [".claude-plugin/marketplace.json", ...exp.plugins.map((p) => `plugins/${p.name}`)]) {
   314	      const r = run(bin, ["plugin", "validate", "--strict", path.join(copy, target)], opts);
   315	      check(`claude: plugin validate --strict ${target}`, r.ok, () => `${r.problem}\n${tail(r.output)}`);
   316	    }
   317	
   318	    step("claude: plugin marketplace add <copy>", bin, ["plugin", "marketplace", "add", copy], opts);
   319	    for (const p of first) step(`claude: plugin install ${p.id}`, bin, ["plugin", "install", p.id], opts);
   320	    checkClaudeInstalled("claude step 1", claudeList(bin, opts, "claude step 1"), first, exp.mkt, configDir);
   321	
   322	    for (const p of second) step(`claude: plugin install ${p.id}`, bin, ["plugin", "install", p.id], opts);
   323	    checkClaudeInstalled("claude step 2", claudeList(bin, opts, "claude step 2"), exp.plugins, exp.mkt, configDir);
   324	  } catch (err) {
   325	    if (!(err instanceof Abort)) fail("claude: unexpected error", err.stack ?? err.message);
   326	  }
   327	}
   328	
   329	// ---------------------------------------------------------------- Codex app-server
   330	
   331	function startAppServer(bin, { cwd, env }) {
   332	  const child = spawn(bin, ["app-server", "--listen", "stdio://"], { cwd, env, stdio: ["pipe", "pipe", "pipe"] });
   333	  children.add(child);
   334	  let stderr = "";
   335	  let exited = null;
   336	  let nextId = 0;
   337	  const pending = new Map();
   338	  const rejectAll = (err) => {
   339	    for (const { reject, timer } of pending.values()) {
   340	      clearTimeout(timer);
   341	      reject(err);
   342	    }
   343	    pending.clear();
   344	  };
   345	  // Drain stderr so a full pipe never blocks the server.
   346	  child.stderr.on("data", (d) => {
   347	    stderr = (stderr + d).slice(-20_000);
   348	  });
   349	  child.stdin.on("error", () => { /* reported through exit */ });
   350	  child.on("error", (err) => {
   351	    exited = { error: err };
   352	    rejectAll(new Error(`app-server failed to start: ${err.message}`));
   353	  });
   354	  child.on("exit", (code, signal) => {
   355	    exited = { code, signal };
   356	    children.delete(child);
   357	    rejectAll(new Error(`app-server exited (${code ?? signal})\n${tail(stderr)}`));
   358	  });
   359	  // One JSON object per line. Responses carry an id and no method; notifications
   360	  // and server requests are ignored.
   361	  readline.createInterface({ input: child.stdout }).on("line", (line) => {
   362	    line = line.trim();
   363	    if (!line.startsWith("{")) return;
   364	    let msg;
   365	    try { msg = JSON.parse(line); } catch { return; }
   366	    if (msg.method !== undefined || !pending.has(msg.id)) return;
   367	    const { resolve, timer } = pending.get(msg.id);
   368	    pending.delete(msg.id);
   369	    clearTimeout(timer);
   370	    resolve(msg);
   371	  });
   372	
   373	  const send = (msg) => child.stdin.write(JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", ...msg }) + "\n");
   374	  return {
   375	    request(method, params, timeout = RPC_TIMEOUT_MS) {
   376	      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   377	        if (exited) return reject(new Error(`app-server is not running (${JSON.stringify(exited)})\n${tail(stderr)}`));
   378	        const id = ++nextId;
   379	        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
   380	          pending.delete(id);
   381	          reject(new Error(`${method} timed out after ${timeout / 1000}s\n${tail(stderr)}`));
   382	        }, timeout);
   383	        pending.set(id, { resolve, reject, timer });
   384	        send({ id, method, params });
   385	      });
   386	    },
   387	    notify(method, params) {
   388	      send(params === undefined ? { method } : { method, params });
   389	    },
   390	    async close() {
   391	      if (exited) return;
   392	      const done = new Promise((resolve) => child.once("exit", resolve));
   393	      child.stdin.end();
   394	      child.kill("SIGTERM");
   395	      const timer = setTimeout(() => child.kill("SIGKILL"), 3000);
   396	      await done;
   397	      clearTimeout(timer);
   398	    },
   399	  };
   400	}
   401	
   402	async function rpc(server, label, method, params, what = method) {
   403	  let msg;
   404	  try {
   405	    msg = await server.request(method, params);
   406	  } catch (err) {
   407	    fail(`${label}: ${what}`, err.message);
   408	    throw new Abort(what);
   409	  }
   410	  if (!check(`${label}: ${what} succeeds`, !msg.error, () => JSON.stringify(msg.error, null, 2))) throw new Abort(what);
   411	  return msg.result ?? {};
   412	}
   413	
   414	async function codexCatalog(bin, copy, exp, env, label) {
   415	  const server = startAppServer(bin, { cwd: copy, env });
   416	  try {
   417	    await rpc(server, label, "initialize", {
   418	      clientInfo: { name: "adhd-unslop-load-check", title: "adhd-unslop load check", version: "0" },
   419	      capabilities: { experimentalApi: true, requestAttestation: false, optOutNotificationMethods: [] },
   420	    });
   421	    server.notify("initialized");
   422	
   423	    // plugin/list: one local marketplace with this name, read from the Codex file.
   424	    const listed = await rpc(server, label, "plugin/list", { cwds: [copy], marketplaceKinds: ["local"] });
   425	    const loadErrors = listed.marketplaceLoadErrors ?? [];
   426	    check(`${label}: plugin/list reports no marketplaceLoadErrors`, loadErrors.length === 0, JSON.stringify(loadErrors, null, 2));
   427	    const ours = (listed.marketplaces ?? []).filter((m) => m.name === exp.mkt);
   428	    check(`${label}: plugin/list shows exactly one marketplace named ${exp.mkt}`, ours.length === 1, `found ${ours.length}: ${JSON.stringify(ours.map((m) => m.path))}`);
   429	    const codexMarketplace = path.join(copy, ".agents", "plugins", "marketplace.json");
   430	    if (ours.length === 1) {
   431	      check(`${label}: ${exp.mkt} is read from .agents/plugins/marketplace.json`, path.resolve(ours[0].path ?? "") === codexMarketplace, `path: ${ours[0].path}`);
   432	      const names = (ours[0].plugins ?? []).map((p) => p.name);
   433	      check(`${label}: ${exp.mkt} lists ${show(exp.plugins.map((p) => p.name))}`, sameSet(names, exp.plugins.map((p) => p.name)), `listed: ${show(names)}`);
   434	    }
   435	
   436	    // plugin/read reads the marketplace entry, installed or not.
   437	    for (const p of exp.plugins) {
   438	      let plugin;
   439	      try {
   440	        plugin = (await rpc(server, label, "plugin/read", { marketplacePath: codexMarketplace, remoteMarketplaceName: null, pluginName: p.name }, `plugin/read ${p.name}`)).plugin ?? {};
   441	      } catch (err) {
   442	        if (err instanceof Abort) continue;
   443	        throw err;
   444	      }
   445	      const skills = (plugin.skills ?? []).map((s) => s.name);
   446	      check(`${label}: ${p.name} skills are ${show(p.fullSkills)}`, skills.length > 0 && sameSet(skills, p.fullSkills), `read: ${show(skills)}`);
   447	      const hooks = (plugin.hooks ?? []).map((h) => h.key);
   448	      check(`${label}: ${p.name} hook keys are ${p.hookKeys.length ? p.hookKeys.map((k) => k.split(":").slice(-2).join(":")).join(" ") : "none"}`, sameSet(hooks, p.hookKeys), `read: ${show(hooks)}\nexpected: ${show(p.hookKeys)}`);
   449	      const version = plugin.summary?.localVersion;
   450	      check(`${label}: ${p.name} version ${p.version}`, version === p.version, `localVersion: ${version}`);
   451	      const displayName = plugin.summary?.interface?.displayName ?? null;
   452	      check(`${label}: ${p.name} interface.displayName from .codex-plugin`, displayName === p.displayName, `displayName: ${JSON.stringify(displayName)}, expected ${JSON.stringify(p.displayName)}`);
   453	    }
   454	
   455	    // hooks/list reports the installed plugins' hooks, from the cache copy.
   456	    const hookList = await rpc(server, label, "hooks/list", { cwds: [copy] });
   457	    const entries = hookList.data ?? [];
   458	    const problems = entries.flatMap((e) => [...(e.warnings ?? []), ...(e.errors ?? [])]);
   459	    check(`${label}: hooks/list reports no warnings or errors`, problems.length === 0, JSON.stringify(problems, null, 2));
   460	    const all = new Map();
   461	    for (const e of entries) for (const h of e.hooks ?? []) all.set(h.key, h);
   462	    const listedHooks = [...all.values()].filter((h) => String(h.pluginId ?? "").endsWith(`@${exp.mkt}`));
   463	    for (const p of exp.plugins) {
   464	      const mine = listedHooks.filter((h) => h.pluginId === p.id);
   465	      check(`${label}: hooks/list for ${p.name}: ${p.hookKeys.length ? p.hookKeys.map((k) => k.split(":").slice(-2).join(":")).join(" ") : "none"}`, sameSet(mine.map((h) => h.key), p.hookKeys), `listed: ${show(mine.map((h) => h.key))}\nexpected: ${show(p.hookKeys)}`);
   466	      if (!mine.length) continue;
   467	      const suffix = ["plugins", "cache", exp.mkt, p.name, p.version, ...p.hooksFile.split("/")].join(path.sep);
   468	      const wrong = mine.filter((h) => !path.resolve(h.sourcePath ?? "").endsWith(path.sep + suffix));
   469	      check(`${label}: hooks/list for ${p.name} reads the ${p.version} cache copy`, wrong.length === 0, wrong.map((h) => `${h.key}: ${h.sourcePath}`).join("\n"));
   470	      const unhashed = mine.filter((h) => !/^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(h.currentHash ?? ""));
   471	      check(`${label}: hooks/list for ${p.name} gives each handler a trust hash`, unhashed.length === 0, unhashed.map((h) => `${h.key}: ${h.currentHash}`).join("\n"));
   472	      console.log(`      trust: ${mine.map((h) => `${h.key.split(":").slice(-2).join(":")}=${h.trustStatus}`).join(" ")}`);
   473	    }
   474	  } finally {
   475	    await server.close();
   476	  }
   477	}

   481	async function runCodex(bin, copy, exp, tmp) {
   482	  const home = path.join(tmp, "codex-home");
   483	  const codexHome = path.join(tmp, "codex-config");
   484	  fs.mkdirSync(home, { recursive: true });
   485	  fs.mkdirSync(codexHome, { recursive: true });
   486	  const env = baseEnv({ HOME: home, USERPROFILE: home, CODEX_HOME: codexHome, CODEX_APP_SERVER_DISABLE_MANAGED_CONFIG: "1", RUST_LOG: "warn" });
   487	  const opts = { env, cwd: copy };
   488	  console.log(`codex: ${cliVersion(bin, env)}`);
   489	
   490	  const cache = path.join(codexHome, "plugins", "cache", exp.mkt);
   491	  const checkCodexInstalled = (label, installed) => {
   492	    checkCacheLayout(label, cache, installed);
   493	    for (const p of installed) {
   494	      const dir = path.join(cache, p.name, p.version);
   495	      if (fs.existsSync(dir)) checkInstalledFiles(label, p, dir);
   496	    }
   497	  };
   498	  const first = exp.plugins.filter((p) => p.kind === "authored");
   499	  const second = exp.plugins.filter((p) => p.kind !== "authored");
   500	  try {
   501	    step("codex: plugin marketplace add <copy>", bin, ["plugin", "marketplace", "add", copy], opts);
   502	    for (const p of first) step(`codex: plugin add ${p.id}`, bin, ["plugin", "add", p.id], opts);
   503	    checkCodexInstalled("codex step 1", first);
   504	
   505	    for (const p of second) step(`codex: plugin add ${p.id}`, bin, ["plugin", "add", p.id], opts);
   506	    checkCodexInstalled("codex step 2", exp.plugins);
   507	
   508	    await codexCatalog(bin, copy, exp, env, "codex catalog");
   509	  } catch (err) {
   510	    if (!(err instanceof Abort)) fail("codex: unexpected error", err.stack ?? err.message);
   511	  }
   512	}

   514	// ---------------------------------------------------------------- main
   515	
   516	async function main() {
   517	  const opts = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
   518	  const tmp = makeTempRoot();
   519	  const copy = path.join(tmp, "marketplace");
   520	  const count = copyRepo(opts.repo, copy);
   521	  console.log(`marketplace copy: ${count} files from ${opts.repo}`);
   522	  const exp = expectations(copy);
   523	  if (!exp.plugins.length) fail("tools/plugins.json lists plugins", "no plugins found");
   524	  if (!exp.plugins.some((p) => p.kind === "authored")) fail("tools/plugins.json lists an authored plugin", "step one would install nothing");
   525	
   526	  if (opts.only !== "codex") runClaude(opts.claude, copy, exp, tmp);
   527	  if (opts.only !== "claude") await runCodex(opts.codex, copy, exp, tmp);
   528	
   529	  console.log(`\n${passed} passed, ${failed} failed`);
   530	  process.exitCode = failed ? 1 : 0;
   531	}
   532	
   533	main().catch((err) => {
   534	  console.error(err.stack ?? err.message);
   535	  process.exitCode = 1;
   536	});

     2	// Keep upstream/ in step with the pins in tools/upstream.json.
     3	//
     4	//   node tools/sync.mjs --check                 verify upstream/ files against pinned sha256
     5	//   node tools/sync.mjs --bump <name> <commit>  fetch <name> at <commit> into .sync-staging/,
     6	//                                               check dependencies and rule citations, swap in,
     7	//                                               raise plugin versions, build and test,
     8	//                                               promote on success, restore on failure
     9	//   node tools/sync.mjs --verify-remote         refetch every pinned file at its pinned commit and
    10	//                                               compare it with the pinned sha256
    11	//   node tools/sync.mjs --latest [--report <file>]
    12	//                                               bump every upstream whose pinned files changed
    13	//                                               on its default branch, write a Markdown report,
    14	//                                               exit 1 if any bump failed
    15	//
    16	// Set GITHUB_TOKEN to avoid the unauthenticated GitHub API rate limit.
    17	// No dependencies. Uses global fetch (Node 18 or later).

    36	export function checkPins(pins = readPins()) {
    37	  const problems = [];
    38	  for (const [name, pin] of Object.entries(pins.upstreams)) {
    39	    for (const [file, meta] of Object.entries(pin.files)) {
    40	      const local = path.join(repo, "upstream", name, file);
    41	      if (!fs.existsSync(local)) { problems.push(`${name}/${file}: missing`); continue; }
    42	      const actual = sha256(read(local));
    43	      if (actual !== meta.sha256) problems.push(`${name}/${file}: sha256 ${actual} != pinned ${meta.sha256}`);
    44	    }
    45	  }
    46	  return problems;
    47	}

    49	// Runtime dependencies the composite cannot ship: relative Markdown links and
    50	// sibling directories a skill would read at run time.
    51	export function dependencyHits(body) {
    52	  const hits = [];
    53	  body.split("\n").forEach((line, i) => {
    54	    if (/\]\((?!https?:|#|mailto:)[^)]+\)/.test(line)) hits.push(`${i + 1}: relative link: ${line.trim()}`);
    55	    if (/(^|[\s`(])(\.\/)?(references|scripts|agents)\//.test(line)) hits.push(`${i + 1}: sibling directory: ${line.trim()}`);
    56	  });
    57	  return hits;
    58	}

    60	// Numbered items in the section that starts with `heading`, up to the next `## `.
    61	function listNumbers(body, heading) {
    62	  const start = body.indexOf(`\n${heading}\n`);
    63	  if (start < 0) return [];
    64	  const rest = body.slice(start + heading.length + 2);
    65	  const end = rest.search(/^## /m);
    66	  const section = end < 0 ? rest : rest.slice(0, end);
    67	  return [...section.matchAll(/^(\d+)\. /gm)].map((m) => Number(m[1]));
    68	}
    69	
    70	// Every citation label the overlay may use, the upstream it points into, and
    71	// how to read the numbers that exist in that upstream body.
    72	export const CITATION_KINDS = [
    73	  { label: "ADHD rule", upstream: "i-have-adhd", numbers: (b) => [...b.matchAll(/^### (\d+)\. /gm)].map((m) => Number(m[1])) },
    74	  { label: "ADHD exception", upstream: "i-have-adhd", numbers: (b) => listNumbers(b, "## When to break the rules") },
    75	  { label: "ADHD check", upstream: "i-have-adhd", numbers: (b) => listNumbers(b, "## Pre-send check") },
    76	  { label: "unslop rule", upstream: "unslop", numbers: (b) => [...b.matchAll(/^(\d+)\. \*\*/gm)].map((m) => Number(m[1])) },
    77	  { label: "unslop process", upstream: "unslop", numbers: (b) => listNumbers(b, "## Process") },
    78	];
    79	
    80	const kindFor = (label) => CITATION_KINDS.find((k) => k.label === label);
    81	const ruleLabel = (name) => (name === "unslop" ? "unslop rule" : "ADHD rule");
    82	
    83	export function ruleNumbers(name, body) {
    84	  return kindFor(ruleLabel(name)).numbers(body);
    85	}
    86	
    87	export function citedNumbers(label) {
    88	  const text = fs.readdirSync(overlayDir).map((f) => read(path.join(overlayDir, f))).join("\n");
    89	  const re = new RegExp(`${label}s? (\\d+)(?:(?:,| and| to) (\\d+))*`, "g");
    90	  const cited = new Set();
    91	  for (const m of text.matchAll(re)) {
    92	    const nums = m[0].match(/\d+/g).map(Number);
    93	    if (/ to /.test(m[0]) && nums.length === 2) for (let n = nums[0]; n <= nums[1]; n++) cited.add(n);
    94	    else nums.forEach((n) => cited.add(n));
    95	  }
    96	  return cited;
    97	}
    98	
    99	export function overlayCitations(name) {
   100	  return citedNumbers(ruleLabel(name));
   101	}
   102	
   103	export function citationReport(name, oldBody, newBody) {
   104	  const warnings = [];
   105	  const failures = [];
   106	  for (const kind of CITATION_KINDS.filter((k) => k.upstream === name)) {
   107	    const oldNums = new Set(kind.numbers(oldBody));
   108	    const newNums = new Set(kind.numbers(newBody));
   109	    const cited = citedNumbers(kind.label);
   110	    if (kind.label === ruleLabel(name)) {
   111	      warnings.push(...[...newNums].filter((n) => !oldNums.has(n) && !cited.has(n)).map((n) => `${kind.label} ${n} is new and not cited in the overlay`));
   112	    }
   113	    failures.push(...[...cited].filter((n) => !newNums.has(n)).map((n) => `the overlay cites ${kind.label} ${n}, which no longer exists upstream`));
   114	  }
   115	  return { warnings, failures };
   116	}

   118	function githubHeaders() {
   119	  const h = { Accept: "application/vnd.github+json", "User-Agent": "adhd-unslop-sync" };
   120	  if (process.env.GITHUB_TOKEN) h.Authorization = `Bearer ${process.env.GITHUB_TOKEN}`;
   121	  return h;
   122	}

   124	async function fetchRaw(repoSlug, commit, filePath) {
   125	  const url = `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/${repoSlug}/${commit}/${filePath}`;
   126	  const res = await fetch(url);
   127	  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${res.status} fetching ${url}`);
   128	  return res.text();
   129	}

   131	// Newest commit on the default branch that touched any pinned file of this upstream.
   132	export async function latestCommit(pin) {
   133	  let newest = null;
   134	  for (const meta of Object.values(pin.files)) {
   135	    const url = `https://api.github.com/repos/${pin.repo}/commits?path=${encodeURIComponent(meta.path)}&per_page=1`;
   136	    const res = await fetch(url, { headers: githubHeaders() });
   137	    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${res.status} fetching ${url}`);
   138	    const [c] = await res.json();
   139	    if (c && (!newest || c.commit.committer.date > newest.date)) newest = { sha: c.sha, date: c.commit.committer.date };
   140	  }
   141	  return newest?.sha ?? null;
   142	}
   143	
   144	export function bumpPatch(version) {
   145	  const m = /^(\d+)\.(\d+)\.(\d+)$/.exec(version);
   146	  if (!m) throw new Error(`cannot bump version ${version}`);
   147	  return `${m[1]}.${m[2]}.${Number(m[3]) + 1}`;
   148	}

   150	// Plugins whose shipped text includes one of these upstreams: its mirror, any
   151	// skill that lists it in references, and any always-on chunk that carries it.
   152	export function pluginsEmbedding(config, names) {
   153	  return config.plugins.filter((p) => upstreamsShipped(p).some((n) => names.includes(n))).map((p) => p.name);
   154	}
   155	
   156	// Raise each affected plugin's patch version once, however many upstreams changed.
   157	export function raiseVersions(config, names) {
   158	  const affected = pluginsEmbedding(config, names);
   159	  for (const p of config.plugins) if (affected.includes(p.name)) p.version = bumpPatch(p.version);
   160	  return config.plugins.filter((p) => affected.includes(p.name)).map((p) => `${p.name} ${p.version}`);
   161	}

   172	const GENERATED = GENERATED_ROOTS;
   173	
   174	// Copy everything a bump can change, and return a function that puts it back.
   175	function snapshot(label) {
   176	  const dir = path.join(staging, `${label}.backup`);
   177	  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   178	  for (const r of [...GENERATED, "upstream", "tools/upstream.json", "tools/plugins.json"]) {
   179	    fs.cpSync(path.join(repo, r), path.join(dir, r), { recursive: true });
   180	  }
   181	  return {
   182	    restore() {
   183	      for (const r of [...GENERATED, "upstream", "tools/upstream.json", "tools/plugins.json"]) {
   184	        fs.rmSync(path.join(repo, r), { recursive: true, force: true });
   185	        fs.cpSync(path.join(dir, r), path.join(repo, r), { recursive: true });
   186	      }
   187	      fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   188	    },
   189	    discard() {
   190	      fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   191	    },
   192	  };
   193	}

   195	async function fetchUpstream(pin, commit) {
   196	  const fetched = {};
   197	  for (const [file, meta] of Object.entries(pin.files)) fetched[file] = await fetchRaw(pin.repo, commit, meta.path);
   198	  return fetched;
   199	}

   201	// Returns { name, from, to, warnings, versions }. Throws on refusal or failure,
   202	// after restoring every file it touched.
   203	export async function bump(name, commit, { versions = true, fetched } = {}) {
   204	  const pins = readPins();
   205	  const pin = pins.upstreams[name];
   206	  if (!pin) throw new Error(`unknown upstream ${name}; known: ${Object.keys(pins.upstreams).join(", ")}`);
   207	  const stageDir = path.join(staging, name);
   208	  fs.rmSync(stageDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   209	  fs.mkdirSync(stageDir, { recursive: true });
   210	
   211	  fetched ??= await fetchUpstream(pin, commit);
   212	  for (const [file, text] of Object.entries(fetched)) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(stageDir, file), text);
   213	  const newBody = stripFrontmatter(fetched["SKILL.md"]);
   214	  const deps = dependencyHits(newBody);
   215	  if (deps.length) {
   216	    throw new Error(`refusing to bump ${name}: new body has runtime dependencies the plugins do not ship:\n  ${deps.join("\n  ")}\nstaged files left in ${stageDir}`);
   217	  }
   218	  const oldBody = stripFrontmatter(read(path.join(repo, "upstream", name, "SKILL.md")));
   219	  const { warnings, failures } = citationReport(name, oldBody, newBody);
   220	  if (failures.length) throw new Error(`refusing to bump ${name}:\n  ${failures.join("\n  ")}\nstaged files left in ${stageDir}`);
   221	
   222	  const backup = snapshot(name);
   223	  const from = pin.commit;
   224	  let raised = [];
   225	  try {
   226	    for (const [file, text] of Object.entries(fetched)) {
   227	      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(repo, "upstream", name, file), text);
   228	      pin.files[file].sha256 = sha256(text);
   229	    }
   230	    pin.commit = commit;
   231	    fs.writeFileSync(pinsPath, JSON.stringify(pins, null, 2) + "\n");
   232	    if (versions) {
   233	      const config = readConfig();
   234	      raised = raiseVersions(config, [name]);
   235	      fs.writeFileSync(configPath, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2) + "\n");
   236	    }
   237	    buildAndTest();
   238	  } catch (err) {
   239	    backup.restore();
   240	    throw new Error(`bump of ${name} failed and was rolled back (${err.message}). Staged files remain in ${stageDir}.`);
   241	  }
   242	  backup.discard();
   243	  fs.rmSync(stageDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   244	  return { name, from, to: commit, warnings, versions: raised };
   245	}
   246	
   247	export async function latest() {
   248	  const pins = readPins();
   249	  const results = [];
   250	  for (const [name, pin] of Object.entries(pins.upstreams)) {
   251	    try {
   252	      const sha = await latestCommit(pin);
   253	      if (!sha || sha === pin.commit) {
   254	        results.push({ name, status: "current" });
   255	        continue;
   256	      }
   257	      const fetched = await fetchUpstream(pin, sha);
   258	      const unchanged = Object.entries(pin.files).every(([file, meta]) => sha256(fetched[file]) === meta.sha256);
   259	      if (unchanged) {
   260	        results.push({ name, status: "unchanged", to: sha });
   261	        continue;
   262	      }
   263	      const r = await bump(name, sha, { versions: false, fetched });
   264	      results.push({ ...r, status: "bumped" });
   265	    } catch (err) {
   266	      results.push({ name, status: "failed", error: err.message });
   267	    }
   268	  }
   269	  const bumped = results.filter((r) => r.status === "bumped").map((r) => r.name);
   270	  let versions = [];
   271	  if (bumped.length) {
   272	    const config = readConfig();
   273	    versions = raiseVersions(config, bumped);
   274	    fs.writeFileSync(configPath, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2) + "\n");
   275	    buildAndTest();
   276	  }
   277	  return { results, versions };
   278	}
   279	
   280	export function reportMarkdown({ results, versions }, pins = readPins()) {
   281	  const lines = ["# Upstream skill updates", "", "| Upstream | Result | Detail |", "| --- | --- | --- |"];
   282	  for (const r of results) {
   283	    const repoSlug = pins.upstreams[r.name].repo;
   284	    if (r.status === "bumped") lines.push(`| ${r.name} | updated | [${r.from.slice(0, 7)}...${r.to.slice(0, 7)}](https://github.com/${repoSlug}/compare/${r.from}...${r.to}) |`);
   285	    else if (r.status === "unchanged") lines.push(`| ${r.name} | no change | ${r.to.slice(0, 7)} changed no pinned file |`);
   286	    else if (r.status === "current") lines.push(`| ${r.name} | no change | pinned commit is the latest |`);
   287	    else lines.push(`| ${r.name} | failed | see below |`);
   288	  }
   289	  if (versions.length) {
   290	    lines.push("", "## New plugin versions", "", ...versions.map((v) => `- ${v}`), "");
   291	    lines.push("Installed copies update only when each plugin is updated. Neither `claude plugin update` nor Codex updates dependencies on their own.");
   292	  }
   293	  const warnings = results.flatMap((r) => r.warnings ?? []);
   294	  if (warnings.length) lines.push("", "## Warnings", "", ...warnings.map((w) => `- ${w}`));
   295	  const failed = results.filter((r) => r.status === "failed");
   296	  if (failed.length) {
   297	    lines.push("", "## Failures", "");
   298	    for (const f of failed) lines.push(`### ${f.name}`, "", "```", f.error, "```", "");
   299	    lines.push("A failure usually means upstream removed a rule the overlay cites. Update `src/adhd-unslop/overlay/`, then run `node tools/sync.mjs --bump <name> <commit>`.");
   300	  }
   301	  lines.push("", "Review the upstream diff for new conflicts and record them in the outcome table in `src/adhd-unslop/overlay/10-precedence.md`.", "");
   302	  return lines.join("\n");
   303	}
   304	
   305	// Refetch each pinned file at its pinned commit and compare it with the pin.
   306	// Catches a hand edit to upstream/ that also rewrote the sha256 in the pin.
   307	export async function verifyRemote(pins = readPins()) {
   308	  const problems = [];
   309	  for (const [name, pin] of Object.entries(pins.upstreams)) {
   310	    for (const [file, meta] of Object.entries(pin.files)) {
   311	      const text = await fetchRaw(pin.repo, pin.commit, meta.path);
   312	      const actual = sha256(text);
   313	      if (actual !== meta.sha256) problems.push(`${name}/${file}: upstream at ${pin.commit} has sha256 ${actual}, pinned ${meta.sha256}`);
   314	    }
   315	  }
   316	  return problems;
   317	}
   318	
   319	if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
   320	  const [, , cmd, ...rest] = process.argv;
   321	  try {
   322	    if (cmd === "--check") {
   323	      const problems = checkPins();
   324	      if (problems.length) { console.error(problems.join("\n")); process.exit(1); }
   325	      console.log("upstream/ matches tools/upstream.json");
   326	    } else if (cmd === "--verify-remote") {
   327	      const problems = await verifyRemote();
   328	      if (problems.length) { console.error(problems.join("\n")); process.exit(1); }
   329	      console.log("every pinned file matches its upstream commit");
   330	    } else if (cmd === "--bump" && rest.length === 2) {
   331	      const r = await bump(rest[0], rest[1]);
   332	      r.warnings.forEach((w) => console.warn(`warning: ${w}`));
   333	      console.log(`promoted ${r.name} to ${r.to}; new versions: ${r.versions.join(", ") || "none"}. Review: git diff`);
   334	    } else if (cmd === "--latest") {
   335	      const i = rest.indexOf("--report");
   336	      const summary = await latest();
   337	      const md = reportMarkdown(summary);
   338	      if (i >= 0 && rest[i + 1]) fs.writeFileSync(rest[i + 1], md);
   339	      console.log(md);
   340	      if (summary.results.some((r) => r.status === "failed")) process.exit(1);
   341	    } else {
   342	      console.error("usage: node tools/sync.mjs --check | --verify-remote | --bump <name> <commit> | --latest [--report <file>]");
   343	      process.exit(2);
   344	    }
   345	  } catch (err) {
   346	    console.error(err.message);
   347	    process.exit(1);
   348	  }
   349	}

     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	// Keep upstream/ in step with the pins in tools/upstream.json.
     3	//
     4	//   node tools/sync.mjs --check                 verify upstream/ files against pinned sha256
     5	//   node tools/sync.mjs --bump <name> <commit>  fetch <name> at <commit> into .sync-staging/,
     6	//                                               check dependencies and rule citations, swap in,
     7	//                                               raise plugin versions, build and test,
     8	//                                               promote on success, restore on failure
     9	//   node tools/sync.mjs --verify-remote         refetch every pinned file at its pinned commit and
    10	//                                               compare it with the pinned sha256
    11	//   node tools/sync.mjs --latest [--report <file>]
    12	//                                               bump every upstream whose pinned files changed
    13	//                                               on its default branch, write a Markdown report,
    14	//                                               exit 1 if any bump failed
    15	//
    16	// Set GITHUB_TOKEN to avoid the unauthenticated GitHub API rate limit.
    17	// No dependencies. Uses global fetch (Node 18 or later).
    18	
    19	import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
    20	import fs from "node:fs";
    21	import path from "node:path";
    22	import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
    23	import { sha256, stripFrontmatter } from "../src/adhd-unslop/hooks/lib.mjs";
    24	import { GENERATED_ROOTS, upstreamsShipped } from "./build.mjs";
    25	
    26	const repo = path.join(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
    27	const pinsPath = path.join(repo, "tools", "upstream.json");
    28	const staging = path.join(repo, ".sync-staging");
    29	const overlayDir = path.join(repo, "src", "adhd-unslop", "overlay");
    30	
    31	const read = (p) => fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8");
    32	const readPins = () => JSON.parse(read(pinsPath));
    33	const configPath = path.join(repo, "tools", "plugins.json");
    34	const readConfig = () => JSON.parse(read(configPath));
    35	
    36	export function checkPins(pins = readPins()) {
    37	  const problems = [];
    38	  for (const [name, pin] of Object.entries(pins.upstreams)) {
    39	    for (const [file, meta] of Object.entries(pin.files)) {
    40	      const local = path.join(repo, "upstream", name, file);
    41	      if (!fs.existsSync(local)) { problems.push(`${name}/${file}: missing`); continue; }
    42	      const actual = sha256(read(local));
    43	      if (actual !== meta.sha256) problems.push(`${name}/${file}: sha256 ${actual} != pinned ${meta.sha256}`);
    44	    }
    45	  }
    46	  return problems;
    47	}
    48	
    49	// Runtime dependencies the composite cannot ship: relative Markdown links and
    50	// sibling directories a skill would read at run time.
    51	export function dependencyHits(body) {
    52	  const hits = [];
    53	  body.split("\n").forEach((line, i) => {
    54	    if (/\]\((?!https?:|#|mailto:)[^)]+\)/.test(line)) hits.push(`${i + 1}: relative link: ${line.trim()}`);
    55	    if (/(^|[\s`(])(\.\/)?(references|scripts|agents)\//.test(line)) hits.push(`${i + 1}: sibling directory: ${line.trim()}`);
    56	  });
    57	  return hits;
    58	}
    59	
    60	// Numbered items in the section that starts with `heading`, up to the next `## `.
    61	function listNumbers(body, heading) {
    62	  const start = body.indexOf(`\n${heading}\n`);
    63	  if (start < 0) return [];
    64	  const rest = body.slice(start + heading.length + 2);
    65	  const end = rest.search(/^## /m);
    66	  const section = end < 0 ? rest : rest.slice(0, end);
    67	  return [...section.matchAll(/^(\d+)\. /gm)].map((m) => Number(m[1]));
    68	}
    69	
    70	// Every citation label the overlay may use, the upstream it points into, and
    71	// how to read the numbers that exist in that upstream body.
    72	export const CITATION_KINDS = [
    73	  { label: "ADHD rule", upstream: "i-have-adhd", numbers: (b) => [...b.matchAll(/^### (\d+)\. /gm)].map((m) => Number(m[1])) },
    74	  { label: "ADHD exception", upstream: "i-have-adhd", numbers: (b) => listNumbers(b, "## When to break the rules") },
    75	  { label: "ADHD check", upstream: "i-have-adhd", numbers: (b) => listNumbers(b, "## Pre-send check") },
    76	  { label: "unslop rule", upstream: "unslop", numbers: (b) => [...b.matchAll(/^(\d+)\. \*\*/gm)].map((m) => Number(m[1])) },
    77	  { label: "unslop process", upstream: "unslop", numbers: (b) => listNumbers(b, "## Process") },
    78	];
    79	
    80	const kindFor = (label) => CITATION_KINDS.find((k) => k.label === label);
    81	const ruleLabel = (name) => (name === "unslop" ? "unslop rule" : "ADHD rule");
    82	
    83	export function ruleNumbers(name, body) {
    84	  return kindFor(ruleLabel(name)).numbers(body);
    85	}
    86	
    87	export function citedNumbers(label) {
    88	  const text = fs.readdirSync(overlayDir).map((f) => read(path.join(overlayDir, f))).join("\n");
    89	  const re = new RegExp(`${label}s? (\\d+)(?:(?:,| and| to) (\\d+))*`, "g");
    90	  const cited = new Set();
    91	  for (const m of text.matchAll(re)) {
    92	    const nums = m[0].match(/\d+/g).map(Number);
    93	    if (/ to /.test(m[0]) && nums.length === 2) for (let n = nums[0]; n <= nums[1]; n++) cited.add(n);
    94	    else nums.forEach((n) => cited.add(n));
    95	  }
    96	  return cited;
    97	}
    98	
    99	export function overlayCitations(name) {
   100	  return citedNumbers(ruleLabel(name));
   101	}
   102	
   103	export function citationReport(name, oldBody, newBody) {
   104	  const warnings = [];
   105	  const failures = [];
   106	  for (const kind of CITATION_KINDS.filter((k) => k.upstream === name)) {
   107	    const oldNums = new Set(kind.numbers(oldBody));
   108	    const newNums = new Set(kind.numbers(newBody));
   109	    const cited = citedNumbers(kind.label);
   110	    if (kind.label === ruleLabel(name)) {
   111	      warnings.push(...[...newNums].filter((n) => !oldNums.has(n) && !cited.has(n)).map((n) => `${kind.label} ${n} is new and not cited in the overlay`));
   112	    }
   113	    failures.push(...[...cited].filter((n) => !newNums.has(n)).map((n) => `the overlay cites ${kind.label} ${n}, which no longer exists upstream`));
   114	  }
   115	  return { warnings, failures };
   116	}
   117	
   118	function githubHeaders() {
   119	  const h = { Accept: "application/vnd.github+json", "User-Agent": "adhd-unslop-sync" };
   120	  if (process.env.GITHUB_TOKEN) h.Authorization = `Bearer ${process.env.GITHUB_TOKEN}`;
   121	  return h;
   122	}
   123	
   124	async function fetchRaw(repoSlug, commit, filePath) {
   125	  const url = `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/${repoSlug}/${commit}/${filePath}`;
   126	  const res = await fetch(url);
   127	  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${res.status} fetching ${url}`);
   128	  return res.text();
   129	}
   130	
   131	// Newest commit on the default branch that touched any pinned file of this upstream.
   132	export async function latestCommit(pin) {
   133	  let newest = null;
   134	  for (const meta of Object.values(pin.files)) {
   135	    const url = `https://api.github.com/repos/${pin.repo}/commits?path=${encodeURIComponent(meta.path)}&per_page=1`;
   136	    const res = await fetch(url, { headers: githubHeaders() });
   137	    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${res.status} fetching ${url}`);
   138	    const [c] = await res.json();
   139	    if (c && (!newest || c.commit.committer.date > newest.date)) newest = { sha: c.sha, date: c.commit.committer.date };
   140	  }
   141	  return newest?.sha ?? null;
   142	}
   143	
   144	export function bumpPatch(version) {
   145	  const m = /^(\d+)\.(\d+)\.(\d+)$/.exec(version);
   146	  if (!m) throw new Error(`cannot bump version ${version}`);
   147	  return `${m[1]}.${m[2]}.${Number(m[3]) + 1}`;
   148	}
   149	
   150	// Plugins whose shipped text includes one of these upstreams: its mirror, any
   151	// skill that lists it in references, and any always-on chunk that carries it.
   152	export function pluginsEmbedding(config, names) {
   153	  return config.plugins.filter((p) => upstreamsShipped(p).some((n) => names.includes(n))).map((p) => p.name);
   154	}
   155	
   156	// Raise each affected plugin's patch version once, however many upstreams changed.
   157	export function raiseVersions(config, names) {
   158	  const affected = pluginsEmbedding(config, names);
   159	  for (const p of config.plugins) if (affected.includes(p.name)) p.version = bumpPatch(p.version);
   160	  return config.plugins.filter((p) => affected.includes(p.name)).map((p) => `${p.name} ${p.version}`);
   161	}
   162	
   163	function run(cmd, args) {
   164	  execFileSync(cmd, args, { cwd: repo, stdio: "inherit" });
   165	}
   166	
   167	function buildAndTest() {
   168	  run("node", ["tools/build.mjs"]);
   169	  run("node", ["--test", ...fs.readdirSync(path.join(repo, "tests")).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".test.mjs")).map((f) => path.join("tests", f))]);
   170	}
   171	
   172	const GENERATED = GENERATED_ROOTS;
   173	
   174	// Copy everything a bump can change, and return a function that puts it back.
   175	function snapshot(label) {
   176	  const dir = path.join(staging, `${label}.backup`);
   177	  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   178	  for (const r of [...GENERATED, "upstream", "tools/upstream.json", "tools/plugins.json"]) {
   179	    fs.cpSync(path.join(repo, r), path.join(dir, r), { recursive: true });
   180	  }
   181	  return {
   182	    restore() {
   183	      for (const r of [...GENERATED, "upstream", "tools/upstream.json", "tools/plugins.json"]) {
   184	        fs.rmSync(path.join(repo, r), { recursive: true, force: true });
   185	        fs.cpSync(path.join(dir, r), path.join(repo, r), { recursive: true });
   186	      }
   187	      fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   188	    },
   189	    discard() {
   190	      fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   191	    },
   192	  };
   193	}
   194	
   195	async function fetchUpstream(pin, commit) {
   196	  const fetched = {};
   197	  for (const [file, meta] of Object.entries(pin.files)) fetched[file] = await fetchRaw(pin.repo, commit, meta.path);
   198	  return fetched;
   199	}
   200	
   201	// Returns { name, from, to, warnings, versions }. Throws on refusal or failure,
   202	// after restoring every file it touched.
   203	export async function bump(name, commit, { versions = true, fetched } = {}) {
   204	  const pins = readPins();
   205	  const pin = pins.upstreams[name];
   206	  if (!pin) throw new Error(`unknown upstream ${name}; known: ${Object.keys(pins.upstreams).join(", ")}`);
   207	  const stageDir = path.join(staging, name);
   208	  fs.rmSync(stageDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   209	  fs.mkdirSync(stageDir, { recursive: true });
   210	
   211	  fetched ??= await fetchUpstream(pin, commit);
   212	  for (const [file, text] of Object.entries(fetched)) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(stageDir, file), text);
   213	  const newBody = stripFrontmatter(fetched["SKILL.md"]);
   214	  const deps = dependencyHits(newBody);
   215	  if (deps.length) {
   216	    throw new Error(`refusing to bump ${name}: new body has runtime dependencies the plugins do not ship:\n  ${deps.join("\n  ")}\nstaged files left in ${stageDir}`);
   217	  }
   218	  const oldBody = stripFrontmatter(read(path.join(repo, "upstream", name, "SKILL.md")));
   219	  const { warnings, failures } = citationReport(name, oldBody, newBody);
   220	  if (failures.length) throw new Error(`refusing to bump ${name}:\n  ${failures.join("\n  ")}\nstaged files left in ${stageDir}`);
   221	
   222	  const backup = snapshot(name);
   223	  const from = pin.commit;
   224	  let raised = [];
   225	  try {
   226	    for (const [file, text] of Object.entries(fetched)) {
   227	      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(repo, "upstream", name, file), text);
   228	      pin.files[file].sha256 = sha256(text);
   229	    }
   230	    pin.commit = commit;
   231	    fs.writeFileSync(pinsPath, JSON.stringify(pins, null, 2) + "\n");
   232	    if (versions) {
   233	      const config = readConfig();
   234	      raised = raiseVersions(config, [name]);
   235	      fs.writeFileSync(configPath, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2) + "\n");
   236	    }
   237	    buildAndTest();
   238	  } catch (err) {
   239	    backup.restore();
   240	    throw new Error(`bump of ${name} failed and was rolled back (${err.message}). Staged files remain in ${stageDir}.`);
   241	  }
   242	  backup.discard();
   243	  fs.rmSync(stageDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   244	  return { name, from, to: commit, warnings, versions: raised };
   245	}
   246	
   247	export async function latest() {
   248	  const pins = readPins();
   249	  const results = [];
   250	  for (const [name, pin] of Object.entries(pins.upstreams)) {
   251	    try {
   252	      const sha = await latestCommit(pin);
   253	      if (!sha || sha === pin.commit) {
   254	        results.push({ name, status: "current" });
   255	        continue;
   256	      }
   257	      const fetched = await fetchUpstream(pin, sha);
   258	      const unchanged = Object.entries(pin.files).every(([file, meta]) => sha256(fetched[file]) === meta.sha256);
   259	      if (unchanged) {
   260	        results.push({ name, status: "unchanged", to: sha });
   261	        continue;
   262	      }
   263	      const r = await bump(name, sha, { versions: false, fetched });
   264	      results.push({ ...r, status: "bumped" });
   265	    } catch (err) {
   266	      results.push({ name, status: "failed", error: err.message });
   267	    }
   268	  }
   269	  const bumped = results.filter((r) => r.status === "bumped").map((r) => r.name);
   270	  let versions = [];
   271	  if (bumped.length) {
   272	    const config = readConfig();
   273	    versions = raiseVersions(config, bumped);
   274	    fs.writeFileSync(configPath, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2) + "\n");
   275	    buildAndTest();
   276	  }
   277	  return { results, versions };
   278	}
   279	
   280	export function reportMarkdown({ results, versions }, pins = readPins()) {
   281	  const lines = ["# Upstream skill updates", "", "| Upstream | Result | Detail |", "| --- | --- | --- |"];
   282	  for (const r of results) {
   283	    const repoSlug = pins.upstreams[r.name].repo;
   284	    if (r.status === "bumped") lines.push(`| ${r.name} | updated | [${r.from.slice(0, 7)}...${r.to.slice(0, 7)}](https://github.com/${repoSlug}/compare/${r.from}...${r.to}) |`);
   285	    else if (r.status === "unchanged") lines.push(`| ${r.name} | no change | ${r.to.slice(0, 7)} changed no pinned file |`);
   286	    else if (r.status === "current") lines.push(`| ${r.name} | no change | pinned commit is the latest |`);
   287	    else lines.push(`| ${r.name} | failed | see below |`);
   288	  }
   289	  if (versions.length) {
   290	    lines.push("", "## New plugin versions", "", ...versions.map((v) => `- ${v}`), "");
   291	    lines.push("Installed copies update only when each plugin is updated. Neither `claude plugin update` nor Codex updates dependencies on their own.");
   292	  }
   293	  const warnings = results.flatMap((r) => r.warnings ?? []);
   294	  if (warnings.length) lines.push("", "## Warnings", "", ...warnings.map((w) => `- ${w}`));
   295	  const failed = results.filter((r) => r.status === "failed");
   296	  if (failed.length) {
   297	    lines.push("", "## Failures", "");
   298	    for (const f of failed) lines.push(`### ${f.name}`, "", "```", f.error, "```", "");
   299	    lines.push("A failure usually means upstream removed a rule the overlay cites. Update `src/adhd-unslop/overlay/`, then run `node tools/sync.mjs --bump <name> <commit>`.");
   300	  }
   301	  lines.push("", "Review the upstream diff for new conflicts and record them in the outcome table in `src/adhd-unslop/overlay/10-precedence.md`.", "");
   302	  return lines.join("\n");
   303	}
   304	
   305	// Refetch each pinned file at its pinned commit and compare it with the pin.
   306	// Catches a hand edit to upstream/ that also rewrote the sha256 in the pin.
   307	export async function verifyRemote(pins = readPins()) {
   308	  const problems = [];
   309	  for (const [name, pin] of Object.entries(pins.upstreams)) {
   310	    for (const [file, meta] of Object.entries(pin.files)) {
   311	      const text = await fetchRaw(pin.repo, pin.commit, meta.path);
   312	      const actual = sha256(text);
   313	      if (actual !== meta.sha256) problems.push(`${name}/${file}: upstream at ${pin.commit} has sha256 ${actual}, pinned ${meta.sha256}`);
   314	    }
   315	  }
   316	  return problems;
   317	}
   318	
   319	if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
   320	  const [, , cmd, ...rest] = process.argv;
   321	  try {
   322	    if (cmd === "--check") {
   323	      const problems = checkPins();
   324	      if (problems.length) { console.error(problems.join("\n")); process.exit(1); }
   325	      console.log("upstream/ matches tools/upstream.json");
   326	    } else if (cmd === "--verify-remote") {
   327	      const problems = await verifyRemote();
   328	      if (problems.length) { console.error(problems.join("\n")); process.exit(1); }
   329	      console.log("every pinned file matches its upstream commit");
   330	    } else if (cmd === "--bump" && rest.length === 2) {
   331	      const r = await bump(rest[0], rest[1]);
   332	      r.warnings.forEach((w) => console.warn(`warning: ${w}`));
   333	      console.log(`promoted ${r.name} to ${r.to}; new versions: ${r.versions.join(", ") || "none"}. Review: git diff`);
   334	    } else if (cmd === "--latest") {
   335	      const i = rest.indexOf("--report");
   336	      const summary = await latest();
   337	      const md = reportMarkdown(summary);
   338	      if (i >= 0 && rest[i + 1]) fs.writeFileSync(rest[i + 1], md);
   339	      console.log(md);
   340	      if (summary.results.some((r) => r.status === "failed")) process.exit(1);
   341	    } else {
   342	      console.error("usage: node tools/sync.mjs --check | --verify-remote | --bump <name> <commit> | --latest [--report <file>]");
   343	      process.exit(2);
   344	    }
   345	  } catch (err) {
   346	    console.error(err.message);
   347	    process.exit(1);
   348	  }
   349	}

     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	// Install this marketplace with the real Claude Code and Codex CLIs, with no
     3	// keys and no sign-in, and check what each CLI loads. No model runs.
     4	//
     5	//   node tools/load-check.mjs [--claude <bin>] [--codex <bin>] [--only claude|codex] [--repo <path>]
     6	//
     7	// The repo's tracked and untracked-not-ignored files are copied into a fresh
     8	// git repo in a temp dir, which serves as the marketplace, so no CLI writes
     9	// into the working tree. Every CLI runs in throwaway homes that are deleted at
    10	// the end. Expected names, versions, and hook keys come from
    11	// tools/plugins.json and the generated plugins/*/hooks/hooks.json.
    12	//
    13	// Step one installs the authored plugins only and asserts that nothing else
    14	// comes with them. Step two installs the vendored plugins by name. Codex also
    15	// gets a catalog check through `codex app-server` (plugin/list, plugin/read,
    16	// hooks/list). Prints PASS and FAIL lines and exits 1 on any failure.

    26	const RPC_TIMEOUT_MS = 60_000;

    31	function parseArgs(argv) {
    32	  const opts = {
    33	    claude: "claude",
    34	    codex: "codex",
    35	    only: null,
    36	    repo: path.join(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), ".."),
    37	  };
    38	  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
    39	    const arg = argv[i];
    40	    const value = () => {
    41	      if (i + 1 >= argv.length) usage(`${arg} needs a value`);
    42	      return argv[++i];
    43	    };
    44	    if (arg === "--claude") opts.claude = value();
    45	    else if (arg === "--codex") opts.codex = value();
    46	    else if (arg === "--repo") opts.repo = value();
    47	    else if (arg === "--only") {
    48	      opts.only = value();
    49	      if (!["claude", "codex"].includes(opts.only)) usage(`--only takes claude or codex, not ${opts.only}`);
    50	    } else if (arg === "-h" || arg === "--help") usage();
    51	    else usage(`unknown argument ${arg}`);
    52	  }
    53	  opts.repo = path.resolve(opts.repo);
    54	  return opts;
    55	}

    65	let passed = 0;

    68	function pass(name) {
    69	  passed++;
    70	  console.log(`PASS  ${name}`);
    71	}
    72	
    73	function fail(name, detail) {
    74	  failed++;
    75	  console.log(`FAIL  ${name}`);
    76	  if (detail) for (const line of String(detail).trimEnd().split("\n")) console.log(`      ${line}`);
    77	}
    78	
    79	function check(name, ok, detail) {
    80	  if (ok) pass(name);
    81	  else fail(name, typeof detail === "function" ? detail() : detail);
    82	  return ok;
    83	}

    85	// Thrown to stop one runtime's checks after a step that later steps need.

    97	function cleanup() {
    98	  for (const child of children) {
    99	    try { child.kill("SIGKILL"); } catch { /* already gone */ }
   100	  }
   101	  // Codex writes shell snapshots with environment variables into CODEX_HOME.
   102	  for (const dir of tempRoots.splice(0)) fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   103	}
   104

   106	for (const signal of ["SIGINT", "SIGTERM"]) process.on(signal, () => process.exit(130));

   118	  const env = { ...process.env };
   119	  for (const key of STRIPPED_ENV) delete env[key];

   123	// Every CLI call except the app-server gets stdin from /dev/null.
   124	function run(bin, args, { env, cwd, timeout = CLI_TIMEOUT_MS } = {}) {
   125	  const r = spawnSync(bin, args, { env, cwd, stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"], encoding: "utf8", timeout, maxBuffer: 64 * 1024 * 1024 });
   126	  const output = `${r.stdout ?? ""}${r.stderr ?? ""}`;
   127	  let problem = null;
   128	  if (r.error) problem = r.error.code === "ETIMEDOUT" ? `timed out after ${timeout / 1000}s` : r.error.message;
   129	  else if (r.status !== 0) problem = `exit ${r.status ?? r.signal}`;
   130	  return { ok: !problem, problem, stdout: r.stdout ?? "", output };
   131	}
   132	
   133	// Run a CLI step that later steps depend on. PASS or FAIL, and abort on FAIL.
   134	function step(name, bin, args, opts) {
   135	  const r = run(bin, args, opts);
   136	  const cmd = [path.basename(bin), ...args].join(" ");
   137	  if (!check(name, r.ok, () => `$ ${cmd}\n${r.problem}\n${tail(r.output)}`)) throw new Abort(name);
   138	  return r;
   139	}
   140	
   141	function git(cwd, args) {
   142	  const r = spawnSync("git", args, { cwd, stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"], encoding: "utf8", maxBuffer: 64 * 1024 * 1024 });
   143	  if (r.status !== 0) throw new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${r.error?.message ?? tail(r.stderr)}`);
   144	  return r.stdout;
   145	}

   147	function cliVersion(bin, env) {
   148	  const r = run(bin, ["--version"], { env, timeout: 60_000 });
   149	  return r.ok ? r.stdout.trim() : `unavailable (${r.problem})`;
   150	}

   153	
   154	// Tracked plus untracked-not-ignored files, as tests/e2e/run.sh copies them.
   155	// Index entries deleted from the working tree are skipped.
   156	function copyRepo(repo, dest) {
   157	  const files = git(repo, ["ls-files", "-co", "--exclude-standard", "-z"]).split("\0").filter(Boolean);
   158	  fs.mkdirSync(dest, { recursive: true });
   159	  let copied = 0;
   160	  for (const rel of new Set(files)) {
   161	    const src = path.join(repo, rel);
   162	    let stat;
   163	    try { stat = fs.lstatSync(src); } catch { continue; }
   164	    const out = path.join(dest, rel);
   165	    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(out), { recursive: true });
   166	    if (stat.isSymbolicLink()) fs.symlinkSync(fs.readlinkSync(src), out);
   167	    else if (stat.isFile()) fs.copyFileSync(src, out);
   168	    else continue;
   169	    copied++;
   170	  }
   171	  const quiet = ["-c", "init.defaultBranch=main", "-c", "user.name=load-check", "-c", "user.email=load-check@example.invalid", "-c", "commit.gpgsign=false", "-c", "core.hooksPath=/dev/null"];
   172	  git(dest, [...quiet, "init", "-q"]);
   173	  git(dest, [...quiet, "add", "-A"]);
   174	  git(dest, [...quiet, "commit", "-q", "--no-verify", "-m", "load-check"]);
   175	  return copied;
   176	}

   180	const snake = (event) => event.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, "$1_$2").toLowerCase();
   181	
   182	function listFiles(dir) {
   183	  const out = [];
   184	  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return out;
   185	  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   186	    const abs = path.join(dir, entry.name);
   187	    if (entry.isDirectory()) out.push(...listFiles(abs));
   188	    else out.push(abs);
   189	  }
   190	  return out;

   193	function expectations(copy) {
   194	  const config = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(copy, "tools", "plugins.json"), "utf8"));
   195	  const mkt = config.marketplace.name;
   196	  const plugins = config.plugins.map((p) => {

   198	    const skills = p.kind === "vendored" ? [p.skill] : Object.keys(p.skills ?? {});
   199	    const required = [];
   200	    for (const skill of skills) required.push(`skills/${skill}/SKILL.md`);
   201	    // The hooks file Codex reads, as the Codex manifest names it, relative to the plugin root.
   202	    let hooksFile = "hooks/hooks.json";
   203	    try {
   204	      const codexManifest = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, ".codex-plugin", "plugin.json"), "utf8"));
   205	      if (typeof codexManifest.hooks === "string") hooksFile = codexManifest.hooks.replace(/^\.\//, "");
   206	    } catch { /* checked by the install steps */ }
   207	    const hookKeys = [];
   208	    const hooksPath = path.join(root, hooksFile);
   209	    if (fs.existsSync(hooksPath)) {
   210	      required.push(hooksFile);
   211	      const hooks = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(hooksPath, "utf8")).hooks ?? {};
   212	      for (const [event, groups] of Object.entries(hooks)) {
   213	        groups.forEach((group, g) => (group.hooks ?? []).forEach((_, h) => hookKeys.push(`${p.name}@${mkt}:${hooksFile}:${snake(event)}:${g}:${h}`)));
   214	      }
   215	    }
   216	    if (p.alwaysOn) {
   217	      required.push("hooks/chunks/manifest.json");
   218	      p.alwaysOn.forEach((_, i) => required.push(`hooks/chunks/${i + 1}.md`));
   219	    }
   220	    const sourceFiles = listFiles(root).map((abs) => path.relative(root, abs).split(path.sep).join("/"));
   221	    return {
   222	      name: p.name,
   223	      id: `${p.name}@${mkt}`,
   224	      kind: p.kind,
   225	      version: p.version,
   226	      skills,
   227	      fullSkills: skills.map((s) => `${p.name}:${s}`),
   228	      displayName: p.codexInterface?.displayName ?? null,
   229	      root,
   230	      required,
   231	      sourceFiles,
   232	      hooksFile,
   233	      hookKeys,
   234	    };
   235	  });
   236	  return { mkt, plugins };
   237	}
